raw_knowledge_statement: Given a projection ${\mathbf{X} \mathbf{W} = \mathbf{Y}}$ with $\mathbf{X}\in  \mathbb{R}^{b\times h}$, $\mathbf{W}\in  \mathbb{R}^{h\times o}$ LoRA computes:\begin{equation}
    \mathbf{Y} = \mathbf{X}\mathbf{W} + s\mathbf{X}\mathbf{L}_1\mathbf{L}_2,
\end{equation}
